# Roblox benchmarks and numbers

_Generated 2026-11-01 08:01 by `claude-library/tools/roblox-kb/build_tools.py` from the topic files in this KB. Do not hand-edit; rebuild instead._

One table of benchmark numbers pulled from every topic file, grouped by metric family. Each row has an official/indie flag, the newest citation date and a timestamped source. **Use official rows for platform mechanics and limits; treat indie rows as anecdotes, not targets.** Rows older than 2024 may be outdated (marked `old`).

Flag: **official** = Roblox Creator Docs / Roblox or Roblox Creators sessions; **mixed** = official and indie agree; **devforum** = DevForum post (staff or community, check the link); **indie** = creator self-reported, unverified. Official 2026 docs override older indie advice. Date = newest citation.

To look up more: `python ~\claude-library\tools\roblox-kb\kbq.py num "<metric>"`.


Counts: 3882 unique rows from 57 topic files (of 3930 raw rows).

> **Supplement 2026-11-01 (maintained in `claude-library/tools/roblox-kb/supplements/`, re-inserted on every rebuild; full detail and trust labels in [[ads-stats-monetisation-playbook]]):**
> - Ads Manager auto-bids (you set budget + duration). Official average cost per play (DevForum staff 2025-08-05): Maximize Plays $0.008, Drive Retention $0.007, Reactivate $0.011 (upgrades the indie-only $0.008). OFFICIAL.
> - Current objective names (Creator Docs ads-manager, fetched 2026-11-01): Plays, Earnings (Limited), Engagement; audiences All / New (never or 180+ days) / Recent (30 days, 10,000+ players) / Lapsed (30-180 days, 20,000+). The 2025-08 names Acquire New Users / Drive Retention / Reactivate are superseded. OFFICIAL.
> - Learning period: docs say first 24 h, DevForum staff 2026-10-02 says 24-48 h and restarts on pause; plan for 48 h. OFFICIAL.
> - DevEx: 0.0038 USD/Robux (0.0054 qualifying 18+ US; 0.0035 before 2025-09-05); indie "0.038" is 10x wrong. Engagement-Based Payouts deprecated 2025-07-24 (Creator Rewards). OFFICIAL.
> - Earnings objective beta games had ARPU >= 6 Robux (H1 2026). OFFICIAL (DevForum 2026-10-02).
> - Retention anchor (third party, secondary, SINGLE-SOURCE): GameAnalytics 2026 report, 500+ Roblox games over 1M MAU, Aug 2025-Jul 2026: D1 median 10.3% (top 1% 22.2%), D7 1.6% (9.1%), D30 0.5% (4.7%); session median 9.8 min; payer conversion 3.80%. Excludes games under 1M MAU.
> - Rewarded video: Roblox publishes NO EPM value (docs 2026-11-01); eligibility 2,000 monthly uniques (docs) replaced the 100K DAU launch rule (2025-07). Influencer pricing: no published price list; anecdotal only.

## How reliable is this sheet (trust audit 2026-11-01)

Items checked: 3968. Labels: OFFICIAL 1266, CORROBORATED 78, SINGLE-SOURCE 2366, CONTRADICTED 2, OUTDATED-UNSURE 256.
Label rules (computed from the Flag column, cite count and date; code in `claude-library/tools/roblox-kb/trust_audit.py`): official = OFFICIAL; mixed or indie+devforum = CORROBORATED; devforum or one indie creator = SINGLE-SOURCE; indie with 2+ non-WEAK creators = CORROBORATED; checklist `xN` with N>=2 = CORROBORATED (topic files can share a source, so weaker than independent creators); a fast-moving fact (ads, DevEx, Premium, fees, eligibility, discovery) with newest date before 2025-11 = OUTDATED-UNSURE (tagged inline). CONTRADICTED = checked against current Creator Docs and found wrong; such rows carry an inline `[AUDIT ...]` note with the corrected value. Rows without an inline tag use the rule above.
Limit: labels describe source structure, not a re-verification of every number. Only the rows in the corrections file were checked against live Creator Docs.
Corrections applied here (28 rows, 14 rules): rewarded-100k-dau x1 -> OUTDATED-UNSURE; premium-payout-old x8 -> OUTDATED-UNSURE; expedited-fee x4 -> OFFICIAL; ad-credit-rate x2 -> OUTDATED-UNSURE; devex-legacy-rate x1 -> OFFICIAL; devex-min-balance x1 -> OFFICIAL; devex-us18-rate x1 -> OFFICIAL; devex-0.038 x2 -> CONTRADICTED; ad-min-budget x1 -> OUTDATED-UNSURE; hep-500-superseded x3 -> OUTDATED-UNSURE; learning-period x1 -> OFFICIAL; premium-payout-general x1 -> OUTDATED-UNSURE; hep-250-25-official x1 -> OFFICIAL; xaiyne-30pct x1 -> SINGLE-SOURCE.
Five shakiest items still in the sheet (fast-moving, numeric, not officially re-checked, oldest first):
  1. Campaign CTR reported as good despite poor retention / above 3% / Indie janisjanis, 2025-10. A high CTR alone does not validate an ad. / indie / 2025-10 / ads-and-sponso... (2025-10)
  2. Second campaign spend / 4,000 Robux / Indie janisjanis, 2025-10. Launched after the first ad. / indie / 2025-10 / ads-and-sponsored-experiences / [janisjanis01 2025-10 1... (2025-10)
  3. Total revenue in about 20 days incl. creator rewards / 971,510 Robux (~$3,686) / bubDEV, self-reported. / indie / 2025-10 / monetisation-other / [bubDEV 2025-10 9:39] (2025-10)
  4. Premium / Creator Rewards / DevEx rates / Median/typical DevEx earnings / $645 (2024); about $1,440 (2025); about 29,000 DevEx developers (about 1% of 3.1M) / mixed / 20... (2025-09)
  5. Playthrough target / over 1% / janisjanis, Sep 2025. / indie / 2025-09 / ads-and-sponsored-experiences / [janisjanis01 2025-09 11:31] (2025-09)
